possível via CLI)\n\n```bash\n# Encontrar o target group do ALB\naws elbv2 describe-target-groups \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" \\\n  --region sa-east-1 \\\n  --query 'TargetGroups[?contains(LoadBalancerArns[0], `amazonia`)].[TargetGroupName,TargetGroupArn,HealthCheckPath]' \\\n  --output table\n\n# Se encontrar, atualizar para usar /healthz-deep.php\n# aws elbv2 modify-target-group --target-group-arn ARN \\\n#   --health-check-path /healthz-deep.php \\\n#   --health-check-interval-seconds 15 \\\n#   --healthy-threshold-count 2 \\\n#   --unhealthy-threshold-count 2\n```\n\n## PASSO 16 — systemd override php-fpm: Restart=always\n\n```bash\n# Criar override do serviço php8.3-fpm\nsudo mkdir -p /etc/systemd/system/php8.3-fpm.service.d/\nsudo tee /etc/systemd/system/php8.3-fpm.service.d/override.conf << 'EOF'\n[Service]\nRestart=always\nRestartSec=3s\nEOF\n\n# Recarregar systemd e topic\naws sns create-topic --name \"concertacao-alerts\" \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" --region sa-east-1\n\n# Subscrever email ao topic\naws sns subscribe \\\n  --topic-arn \"ARN_DO_TOPIC\" \\\n  --protocol email \\\n  --notification-endpoint \"daniel.cambria@bureau-it.com\" \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" --region sa-east-1\n```\n\n### 18b — Criar alarmes CloudWatch\n\n```bash\n# Alarme 1: CPU WARNING (>70% por 5 minutos)\naws cloudwatch put-metric-alarm \\\n  --alarm-name \"concertacao-cpu-warning\" \\\n  --alarm-description \"CPU > 70% por 5min — possivel ataque ou sobrecarga\" \\\n  --metric-name CPUUtilization \\\n  --namespace AWS/EC2 \\\n  --statistic Average \\\n  --period 300 \\\n  --threshold 70 \\\n  --comparison-operator GreaterThanThreshold \\\n  --evaluation-periods 1 \\\n  --dimensions Name=InstanceId,Value=i-0c8178fe7ee985cc9 \\\n  --alarm-actions \"ARN_DO_TOPIC\" \\\n  --ok-actions \"ARN_DO_TOPIC\" \\\n  --treat-missing-data notBreaching \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" --region sa-east-1\n\n# Alarme 2: CPU CRITICAL (>85% por 2 minutos)\naws cloudwatch put-metric-alarm \\\n  --alarm-name \"concertacao-cpu-critical\" \\\n  --alarm-description \"CPU > 85% por 2min — CRITICO, risco de downtime iminente\" \\\n  --metric-name CPUUtilization \\\n  --namespace AWS/EC2 \\\n  --statistic Average \\\n  --period 120 \\\n  --threshold 85 \\\n  --comparison-operator GreaterThanThreshold \\\n  --evaluation-periods 1 \\\n  --dimensions Name=InstanceId,Value=i-0c8178fe7ee985cc9 \\\n  --alarm-actions \"ARN_DO_TOPIC\" \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" --region sa-east-1\n\n# Alarme 3: Disco / > 80% (usando CWAgent se disponível, ou métrica customizada)\n# Verificar se CloudWatch Agent está instalado:\naws cloudwatch list-metrics \\\n  --namespace CWAgent \\\n  --dimensions Name=InstanceId,Value=i-0c8178fe7ee985cc9 \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" --region sa-east-1 | head -30\n```\n\nSe CWAgent não estiver instalado, documentar que o alarme de disco requer instalação do CloudWatch Agent.\n\n### 18c — Alarme 5XX CloudFront (já pode existir — verificar e melhorar)\n\n```bash\n# Verificar alarmes existentes para CloudFront\naws cloudwatch describe-alarms \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" --region us-east-1 \\\n  --alarm-name-prefix \"concertacao\" \\\n  --output table 2>/dev/null | head -40\n```\n\n## PASSO 19 — Custom Header X-Origin-Verify (proteção estrutural anti CDN fronting)\n\nEsta é a solução estrutural para CDN fronting attacks: o CloudFront injeta um header secreto em todas as requests ao origin, e o nginx rejeita qualquer request sem esse header.\n\n### 19a — Gerar o segredo\n\n```bash\n# Gerar UUID aleatório seguro\nSECRET=$(python3 -c \"import uuid; print(str(uuid.uuid4()))\")\necho \"Secret gerado: $SECRET\"\n```\n\n### 19b — Configurar o header no CloudFront via CLI\n\n```bash\n# Criar origin request policy com o header customizado\n# Primeiro, verificar policies existentes\naws cloudfront list-origin-request-policies \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" \\\n  --query 'OriginRequestPolicyList.Items[?OriginRequestPolicyConfig.Name!=null].[OriginRequestPolicyConfig.Name,Id]' \\\n  --output table 2>/dev/null | head -20\n\n# Verificar se existe custom header na distribuição atual\naws cloudfront get-distribution-config \\\n  --id E2F1QD7E7YOYEB \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" \\\n  --query 'DistributionConfig.Origins.Items[0].CustomHeaders' \\\n  --output json\n```\n\nSe não existir custom header no origin, adicionar via update-distribution é complexo (requer o config completo). Neste caso:\n\n1. Documentar o processo para implementação manual via console\n2. Gerar o segredo e salvá-lo em arquivo seguro\n3. Preparar o snippet nginx para validar o header\n\n### 19c — Snippet nginx para validar o header (preparar para deploy posterior)\n\nCriar arquivo `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/docs/x-origin-verify-setup.md` documentando:\n\n```\n# X-Origin-Verify Setup\n\n## Proteção estrutural anti CDN fronting attack\n\n### 1. No CloudFront (console AWS):\n- Distribution E2F1QD7E7YOYEB\n- Origins → Edit origin\n- Add custom header:\n  - Header name: X-Origin-Verify\n  - Value: <SECRET_GERADO>\n\n### 2. No nginx (adicionar ao vhost):\nif ($http_x_origin_verify != \"<SECRET_GERADO>\") {\n    return 403;\n}\n# Colocar após os blocos de allow/deny existentes, antes do location /\n\n### 3. Exceções necessárias:\n- Health check ALB (/healthz, /healthz-deep.php): permitir sem header\n  pois o ALB não passa pelo CloudFront\n```\n\nSalvar o SECRET gerado em `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/.x-origin-verify.secret` (não commitar este arquivo).\n\nRetorne:\n- ARN do SNS topic criado ou encontrado\n- Lista de alarmes CloudWatch criados\n- O SECRET gerado para X-Origin-Verify\n- Status de cada operação\n"

--path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br plugin get the-events-calendar --fields=name,version,status 2>&1\n\necho '=== Eventos no banco ==='\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br post list --post_type=tribe_events --posts_per_page=5 --fields=ID,post_title,post_status --format=table 2>&1 | head -20\n\necho '=== URLs TEC no nginx access vs misses recentes para URLs de eventos ==='\nsudo tail -n 1000 /var/log/nginx/access.log 2>/dev/null | grep -iE '/events|/eventos|tribe|/[0-9]{4}/[0-9]{2}/' | awk '{print \\$9, \\$7}' | sort | uniq -c | sort -rn | head -30\n\necho '=== Redis keys relacionadas ao TEC ==='\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br eval '\nglobal \\$wp_object_cache;\nif P17: RateLimit-WPLogin-POST (RateBased 50req/300s, Method POST, URI /wp-login.php)\n\n### nginx em produção (via SSH: concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa)\nVerificar:\n- xmlrpc.php retorna 444 (ou CloudFront converte em 503)\n- debug.log retorna 403 (nova regra)\n- Página de manutenção: /manutencao.html existe e é servida\n- fastcgi_connect_timeout está em 5s no snippet php-fpm.conf\n- error_page 502 503 504 configurada\n\n### Testes funcionais\n```bash\n# xmlrpc.php deve estar bloqueado (503 via CloudFront ou conexão recusada)\ncurl -sI -X POST https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/xmlrpc.php | head -5\n\n# debug.log deve retornar 403 ou 404\ncurl -sI https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/debug.log | head -3\n\n# Homepage deve continuar funcionando\ncurl -sI https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/ | head -5\n\n# wp-login.php GET deve funcionar (apenas POST é rate-limited)\ncurl -sI https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/wp-login.php | head -5\n\n# healthz-deep deve retornar healthy\ncurl -s https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/healthz-deep.php | python3 -m json.tool\n\n# Manutencao.html deve ser servida\ncurl -sI https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/manutencao.html | head -5\n```\n\n### mu-plugin v1.1.0\nVerificar via WP-CLI:\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br eval '\n// Verificar hooks ativos\necho \\\"xmlrpc_enabled: \\\" . has_filter(\\\"xmlrpc_enabled\\\") . PHP_EOL;\necho \\\"login_errors: \\\" . has_filter(\\\"login_errors\\\") . PHP_EOL;\necho \\\"rest_endpoints: \\\" . has_filter(\\\"rest_endpoints\\\") . PHP_EOL;\necho \\\"wp_generator removed: \\\" . (!has_action(\\\"wp_head\\\", \\\"wp_generator\\\") ? \\\"yes\\\" : \\\"no\\\") . PHP_EOL;\n// Verificar versão do plugin\n\\$plugin_file = WP_CONTENT_DIR . \\\"/mu-plugins/bit-security-hardening.php\\\";\nif (file_exists(\\$plugin_file)) {\n    \\$data = get_file_data(\\$plugin_file, [\\\"Version\\\" => \\\"Version\\\"]);\n    echo \\\"plugin version: \\\" . \\$data[\\\"Version\\\"] . PHP_EOL;\n}\n' 2>&1\n\"\n```\n\n### systemd override php-fpm\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"sudo systemctl show php8.3-fpm --property=Restart,RestartUSec\"\n```\n\n### ALB health check\n```bash\naws elbv2 describe-target-groups \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" \\\n  --region sa-east-1 \\\n  --query 'TargetGroups[?TargetGroupName==`concertacao-prod-tg`].[HealthCheckPath,HealthCheckIntervalSeconds,HealthyThresholdCount,UnhealthyThresholdCount]' \\\n  --output table\n```\n\n### CloudWatch alarms\n```bash\naws cloudwatch describe-alarms \\\n  --profile \"Concertação\" \\\n  --region sa-east-1 \\\n  --alarm-name-prefix \"concertacao\" \\\n  --query 'MetricAlarms[*].[AlarmName,StateValue,Threshold,ComparisonOperator]' \\\n  --output table\n```\n\nExecute todos os testes e retorne:\n1. Status de cada item (OK / FALHA / AVISO)\n2. Qualquer discrepância encontrada entre o esperado e o observado\n3. Problemas que precisam de ação adicional\n"
}

Dados do servidor\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"\necho '=== TEC versão ==='\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br plugin list --name=the-events-calendar --fields=name,version,status 2>&1\n\necho '=== OPcache status ==='\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br eval '\n\\$s = opcache_get_status(false);\nif (\\$s === false) {\n    echo \\\"OPcache: DESABILITADO no FPM\\n\\\";\n} else {\n    echo \\\"OPcache: ATIVO\\n\\\";\n    echo \\\"Memory used: \\\" . round(\\$s[\\\"memory_usage\\\"][\\\"used_memory\\\"]/1024/1024, 1) . \\\"MB\\n\\\";\n    echo \\\"Memory free: \\\" . round(\\$s[\\\"memory_usage\\\"][\\\"free_memory\\\"]/1024/1024, 1) . \\\"MB\\n\\\";\n    echo \\\"Hits: \\\" . \\$s[\\\"opcache_statistics\\\"][\\\"hits\\\"] . \\\"\\n\\\";\n    echo \\\"Misses: \\\" . \\$s[\\\"opcache_statistics\\\"][\\\"misses\\\"] . \\\"\\n\\\";\n    echo \\\"Cached scripts: \\\" . \\$s[\\\"opcache_statistics\\\"][\\\"num_cached_scripts\\\"] . \\\"\\n\\\";\n}\n' 2>&1\n\necho '=== WP Rocket: cache de eventos existe? ==='\nsudo find /var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/ \\\n  -name 'index.html' 2>/dev/null | wc -l\n\nsudo find /var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/ \\\n  -name 'index.html' -path '*event*' 2>/dev/null | head -10\n\necho '=== WP Rocket: está ativo como network plugin? ==='\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br plugin get rocket --fields=name,status,version 2>&1\n\necho '=== WP Rocket settings (cache enabled?) ==='\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br eval '\n\\$s = get_option(\\\"rocket_settings\\\");\necho \\\"cache_mobile: \\\" . (\\$s[\\\"cache_mobile\\\"] ?? \\\"n/a\\\") . \\\"\\n\\\";\necho \\\"cache_logged_user: \\\" . (\\$s[\\\"cache_logged_user\\\"] ?? \\\"n/a\\\") . \\\"\\n\\\";\necho \\\"reject_uri count: \\\" . count(\\$s[\\\"cache_reject_uri\\\"] ?? []) . \\\"\\n\\\";\necho \\\"cache_query_strings: \\\" . implode(\\\",\\\", \\$s[\\\"cache_query_strings\\\"] ?? []) . \\\"\\n\\\";\n// Verificar se /events/ está na lista de exclusão\n\\$uris = \\$s[\\\"cache_reject_uri\\\"] ?? [];\n\\$event_excluded = array_filter(\\$uris, function(\\$u) { return strpos(\\$u, \\\"event\\\") !== false || strpos(\\$u, \\\"tribe\\\") !== false; });\necho \\\"event URIs excluidas: \\\" . implode(\\\", \\\", \\$event_excluded) . \\\"\\n\\\";\n' 2>&1\n\necho '=== URLs TEC mais acessadas (últimas 2000 linhas do access log) ==='\nsudo tail -n 2000 /var/log/nginx/access.log 2>/dev/null | \\\n  grep -iE '/events|/eventos|tribe|eventDisplay|tribe-bar-date' | \\\n  awk '{print \\$7}' | cut -d'?' -f1 | sort | uniq -c | sort -rn | head -20\n\necho '=== Query strings TEC mais frequentes ==='\nsudo tail -n 2000 /var/log/nginx/access.log 2>/dev/null | \\\n  grep -iE 'tribe-bar-date|eventDisplay|eventDate' | \\\n  awk '{print \\$7}' | grep '?' | sed 's/.*?//' | sort | uniq -c | sort -rn | head -20\n\necho '=== OPcache ini settings ==='\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br eval '\necho ini_get(\\\"opcache.enable\\\") ? \\\"opcache.enable: 1\\n\\\" : \\\"opcache.enable: 0\\n\\\";\necho ini_get(\\\"opcache.enable_cli\\\") ? \\\"opcache.enable_cli: 1\\n\\\" : \\\"opcache.enable_cli: 0\\n\\\";\necho \\\"opcache.memory_consumption: \\\" . ini_get(\\\"opcache.memory_consumption\\\") . \\\"\\n\\\";\necho \\\"opcache.max_accelerated_files: \\\" . ini_get(\\\"opcache.max_accelerated_files\\\") . \\\"\\n\\\";\n' 2>&1\n\"\n```\n\n### 2b. Verificar PHP-FPM OPcache (o WP-CLI pode usar CLI, não FPM)\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"\n# OPcache no FPM pode estar diferente do CLI\nsudo cat /etc/php/8.3/fpm/conf.d/10-opcache.ini 2>/dev/null || \\\nsudo cat /etc/php/8.3/fpm/php.ini 2>/dev/null | grep -i opcache | head -10 || \\\nsudo php8.3 -i | grep -i 'opcache.enable' | head -5\n\"\n```\n\n### 2c. Verificar se WP Rocket está funcionando via header X-Cache\n```bash\ncurl -sI 'https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/' | grep -iE 'x-cache|x-rocket|cf-cache|age'\ncurl -sI 'https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/events/' 2>/dev/null | grep -iE 'x-cache|x-rocket|cf-cache|age' || echo 'URL /events/ nao existe ou retornou redirect'\n```\n\nRetorne:\n1. mu-plugin v1.1.0: lista de correções confirmadas no arquivo\n2. > AGENT

Found the root cause: **OPcache's `zend_extension=opcache` is commented out in php.ini** (`/etc/php/8.3/fpm/php.ini:;zend_extension=opcache`). The `conf.d/99-wordpress.ini` sets `opcache.enable=1` but the extension itself isn't loaded. Also WP Rocket has no `rocket_options` stored — meaning it's never been configured. Let me fix both issues:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_019bBqZ6VP9XsjnfhkUyHKGu
```json
{
  "command": "ssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"\necho '=== Verificar php.ini fpm linha do opcache ===';\nsudo grep -n 'zend_extension.*opcache\\|opcache' /etc/php/8.3/fpm/php.ini | head -10;\necho '';\necho '=== Verificar conf.d - qual arquivo carrega a extensao? ===';\nsudo ls /etc/php/8.3/fpm/conf.d/ | grep -i opcache;\nsudo cat /etc/php/8.3/fpm/conf.d/10-opcache.ini 2>/dev/null;\necho '';\necho '=== CLI tem opcache ativo? ===';\nphp8.3 -r 'var_dump(function_exists(\\\"opcache_get_status\\\") ? opcache_get_status()[\\\"opcache_enabled\\\"] : \\\"N/A\\\");' 2>/dev/null;\necho '';\necho '=== FPM worker count e memoria ===';\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br eval '\n\\$s = opcache_get_status(false);\necho \\\"OPcache enabled: \\\" . (\\$s ? var_export(\\$s[\\\"opcache_enabled\\\"],true) : \\\"false (extension nao ativa)\\\") . \\\"\\n\\\";\necho \\\"memory_limit: \\\" . ini_get(\\\"memory_limit\\\") . \\\"\\n\\\";\necho \\\"max_execution_time: \\\" . ini_get(\\\"max_execution_time\\\") . \\\"\\n\\\";\n' 2>/dev/null;\n\" 2>&1",

> AGENT

**Important finding**: The extension IS loaded (`[53] => Zend OPcache`), but `opcache_get_status()` returns `false`. This means OPcache is loaded as an extension but **opcache.enable=1 is being overridden somewhere** or there's a `restrict_api` issue with the running process. Let me check more carefully:

> TOOL

=== nginx serve arquivos estaticos de cache? ===
    # ==== WP Rocket: serve cache estático sem PHP ====
    # Detecta se o visitante está logado ou tem cookie de carrinho
    set $rocket_bypass 0;
    set $rocket_bypass_reason "";

    # Bypassar para usuários logados
--
    # Caminho do cache: /wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/{host}/{uri}/index.html
    set $rocket_cache_file "/wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/$http_host$uri/index.html";

    # Servir cache estático quando disponível e não há bypass
    location / {
        # Tenta servir: arquivo estático, cache WP Rocket, depois PHP
        try_files $uri $uri/ @rocket_cache;
    }

    location @rocket_cache {
        # Se bypass ativo, vai direto ao PHP
        if ($rocket_bypass = 1) {
            rewrite ^ /index.php last;
        }
        # Tenta o arquivo de cache, senão PHP
        try_files $rocket_cache_file /index.php?$args;
    }

    # WordPress Multisite subdirectory rewrites
    if (!-e $request_filename) {
        rewrite /wp-admin$ $scheme://$host$uri/ permanent;
--
    # Usado pelo ALB — não cachear, não indexar
```

				$rocket_path . 'inc/classes/logger/class-stream-handler.php',
			],
			'WP_Rocket\\Traits\\Memoize'         => [ $rocket_path . 'inc/classes/traits/trait-memoize.php' ],
		];

		if ( isset( $rocket_classes[ $class ] ) ) {
			$file_options = $rocket_classes[ $class ];
			$file         = '';

			foreach ( $file_options as $file_option ) {
				if ( file_exists( $file_option ) ) {
					$file = $file_option;
					break;
				}
			}
		} elseif ( strpos( $class, 'WP_Rocket\\Dependencies\\Monolog\\' ) === 0 ) {
			$class = str_replace( 'WP_Rocket\\Dependencies\\Monolog\\', '', $class );

			$file = $rocket_path . 'inc/Dependencies/Monolog/' . str_replace( '\\', '/', $class ) . '.php';
			if ( ! file_exists( $file ) ) {
				$file = $rocket_path . 'vendor/monolog/monolog/src/' . str_replace( '\\', '/', $class ) . '.php';
			}
		} elseif ( strpos( $class, 'WP_Rocket\\Dependencies\\Psr\\Log\\' ) === 0 ) {
			$class = str_replace( 'WP_Rocket\\Dependencies\\Psr\\Log\\', '', $class );

			$file = $rocket_path . 'inc/Dependencies/Psr/Log/' . str_replace( '\\', '/', $class ) . '.php';
			if ( ! file_exists( $file ) ) {
				$file = $rocket_path . 'vendor/psr/log/' . str_replace( '\\', '/', $class ) . '.php';
			}
		} else {
			return;
		}

		if ( file_exists( $file ) ) {
			require $file;
		}
	}
);

if ( ! class_exists( '\WP_Rocket\Buffer\Cache' ) ) {
	if ( ! defined( 'DONOTROCKETOPTIMIZE' ) ) {
		define( 'DONOTROCKETOPTIMIZE', true ); // phpcs:ignore WordPress.NamingConventions.PrefixAllGlobals.NonPrefixedConstantFound
	}
	return;
}

$rocket_config_class = new Config(
	[
		'config_dir_path' => $rocket_config_path,
	]
);

( new Cache(
	new Tests(
		$rocket_config_class
	),
	$rocket_config_class,
	[
		'cache_dir_path' => $rocket_cache_path,
	]
) )->maybe_init_process();
```

> AGENT

**Found the root cause of WP Rocket not caching**: The `wp-rocket-config/` directory has **no domain config file**. WP Rocket creates per-domain config files like `concertacaoamazonia.com.br.php`. Without this file, `$rocket_config_class` has no config to load, and `maybe_init_process()` returns early without caching.

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01TtxHBRVyJPYnqhdFxnCUm2

  "command": "ssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"\necho '=== WP Rocket config files para este domínio ===';\nsudo find /var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/wp-content/wp-rocket-config/ -name '*.php' 2>/dev/null | head -20;\necho '';\necho '=== Regenerar config via WP Rocket ===';\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br eval '\n// WP Rocket updates config on save settings\n// Trigger via action\nif (function_exists(\\\"rocket_generate_config_file\\\")) {\n    echo \\\"Calling rocket_generate_config_file...\\n\\\";\n    rocket_generate_config_file();\n    echo \\\"Done.\\n\\\";\n} else {\n    echo \\\"Function rocket_generate_config_file not found.\\n\\\";\n    // Try alternative\n    do_action(\\\"rocket_generate_config_file\\\");\n    echo \\\"Tried do_action rocket_generate_config_file\\n\\\";\n}\n' 2>/dev/null;\necho '';\necho '=== Config files após regeneração ===';\nsudo find /var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/wp-content/wp-rocket-config/ -name '*.php' 2>/dev/null;\n\" 2>&1",

}

**Critical finding**: WP Rocket preload has **1,894 failed + 4,728 pending** URLs. The `failed` status for the preload means WP Rocket is trying to preload via HTTP internally but something is blocking it. Let me investigate:

> TOOL

<?php
/**
 * Plugin Name: BIT Loopback SVG Fix
 * Description: Corrige resolução de SVGs no jet-inline-svg quando o site é
 *              acessado via Cloudflare Tunnel.
 *
 *              Problema: jet-elements usa str_replace(site_url('/'), ABSPATH, $url)
 *              para converter URL → path do filesystem. Quando o tunnel está ativo,
 *              tunnel-url-rewrite.php faz site_url() retornar a URL do tunnel
 *              (concertacao.bureau-it.com), mas o DB armazena a URL local
 *              (cambrasmax.local:8484). O str_replace não encontra match →
 *              file_exists falha → fallback <img> sem dimensões.
 *
 *              Solução: filtro prioridade 999 que desfaz o tunnel rewrite em
 *              site_url() e option_siteurl/option_home. O ob_start do
 *              tunnel-url-rewrite.php converte cambrasmax.local → tunnel URL
 *              no HTML de saída, então o browser continua vendo URLs corretas.
 *
 * Version:     1.2.0
 * Author:      Bureau IT
```

> AGENT

**Found the critical issue**: `wp_remote_get('https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/...')` times out. This means the EC2 server **can't make outbound HTTPS requests to itself** (a common NAT loop issue on AWS). WP Rocket preload uses `wp_remote_get` with the public HTTPS URL, which routes through CloudFront → ALB → EC2, creating a loopback that AWS Security Groups may block.

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01F15iU5yn49Jbpcd2jqGQXm
```json
{
  "command": "ssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"\necho '=== Teste de conectividade: EC2 → CloudFront → self ===';\ntimeout 5 curl -s -I https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/ 2>&1 | head -3 || echo 'TIMEOUT ou ERRO';\necho '';\necho '=== Testar conexao direta ao ALB ===';\nALB_IP=\\$(getent hosts amazonia-alb-2100779539.sa-east-1.elb.amazonaws.com 2>/dev/null | awk '{print \\$1}' | head -1);\necho \\\"ALB IP: \\${ALB_IP}\\\";\ntimeout 3 curl -s -I -H 'Host: concertacaoamazonia.com.br' \\\"http://\\${ALB_IP}/sobre-nos/\\\" 2>&1 | head -5 || echo 'TIMEOUT';\necho '';\necho '=== WP Rocket: forcar preload com sslverify=false ===';\nsudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br eval '\n\\$response = wp_remote_get(\\\"https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/sobre-nos/\\\", [\n    \\\"timeout\\\" => 5,\n    \\\"sslverify\\\" => false,\n]);\nif (is_wp_error(\\$response)) {\n    echo \\\"ERROR: \\\" . \\$response->get_error_message() . \\\"\\n\\\";\n} else {\n    echo \\\"HTTP status: \\\" . wp_remote_retrieve_response_code(\\$response) . \\\"\\n\\\";\n}\n' 2>/dev/null;\necho '';\necho '=== Verificar se WP Rocket tem filtro para preload via local ===';\nsudo grep -r 'preload\\|loopback\\|127.0.0.1' /var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/wp-content/mu-plugins/ 2>/dev/null | grep -v '.bak\\|.php~' | head -10;\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Test EC2 loopback connectivity and WP Rocket preload"
}
```

> AGENT

`sslverify=false` works (200). The issue is WP Rocket's default `sslverify=true` times out for self-requests. EC2 can reach CloudFront but SSL verification of the CloudFront cert from inside fails/times out for some reason. The fix is to add a WordPress filter to disable `sslverify` for loopback requests from this host:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01UMVnspJKHNnM8eUYj2foEM
```json
{
  "command": "ssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"\necho '=== Testar: por que sslverify=true falha? ===';\ntimeout 10 curl -s -I https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/ 2>&1 | head -5;\necho '';\necho '=== Tentar com SNI explícito ===';\ntimeout 5 curl -s -I --resolve 'concertacaoamazonia.com.br:443:13.32.1.1' https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/ 2>&1 | head -3 || echo 'TIMEOUT/ERRO';\necho '';\necho '=== Verificar se ec2 consegue resolver DNS ===';\nnslookup concertacaoamazonia.com.br | head -10;\necho '';\necho '=== curl verbose para ver SSL error ===';\ntimeout 8 curl -v https://concertacaoamazonia.com.br/ 2>&1 | grep -E 'SSL|TLS|curl|error|certificate|timeout|connect' | head -20;\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Debug SSL verification failure for self-requests"
}
```

> AGENT

Curl works fine from EC2 with SSL — the issue is PHP's `wp_remote_get` specifically times out. This is likely the WordPress HTTP API using a different SSL stack (libcurl via PHP's cURL extension) that may have issues with the CloudFront certificate chain in this particular environment. The fix is to add a `http_request_args` filter to disable sslverify for loopback requests:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01Xdyc2TmyqCfJHbnAedaH2V
```json
{

> AGENT

PHP cURL works fine with SSL. The timeout in `wp_remote_get` must be from the WP HTTP API using a different mechanism or the timeout is hit during the slow DB query. Let me check the exact WP timeout behavior and then add the `sslverify` fix as a mu-plugin:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash

`wp_remote_get` with `sslverify=true` and `timeout=30` works in **0.1s**! The previous timeout with `timeout=10` was clearly a transient issue (perhaps the site was under load). WP Rocket preload should be working correctly now that the config file exists. The `failed` entries in the queue were from before the config was regenerated. > AGENT

```
server {
    listen 80;
    server_name concertacaoamazonia.com.br;
    root /var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br;
    index index.php index.html;
    include /etc/nginx/snippets/security-headers.conf;

    add_header Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains" always;
    client_max_body_size 128M;

    # Fallback: página de manutenção quando PHP-FPM não responde
    error_page 502 503 504 /manutencao.html;
    location = /manutencao.html {
        root /var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br;
        internal;
        access_log off;
    }

    # ==== WP Rocket: serve cache estático sem PHP ====
    # Detecta se o visitante está logado ou tem cookie de carrinho
    set $rocket_bypass 0;
    set $rocket_bypass_reason "";

    # Bypassar para usuários logados
    if ($http_cookie ~* "wordpress_logged_in_") {
        set $rocket_bypass 1;
        set $rocket_bypass_reason "logged_in";
    }

    # Bypassar para requisições POST
    if ($request_method = POST) {
        set $rocket_bypass 1;
        set $rocket_bypass_reason "post_method";
    }

    # Bypassar para query strings (exceto as permitidas pelo WP Rocket)
    if ($query_string != "") {
        set $rocket_bypass 1;
        set $rocket_bypass_reason "query_string";
    }

    # Bypassar parâmetros aceitos pelo WP Rocket (JetEngine filters)
    if ($query_string ~* "^(eixo|jsf|jet-engine|tax)=") {
        set $rocket_bypass 0;
    }

    # Caminho do cache: /wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/{host}/{uri}/index.html
    set $rocket_cache_file "/wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/$http_host$uri/index.html";

    # Servir cache estático quando disponível e não há bypass
    location / {
        # Tenta servir: arquivo estático, cache WP Rocket, depois PHP
        try_files $uri $uri/ @rocket_cache;
    }

    location @rocket_cache {
        # Se bypass ativo, vai direto ao PHP
        if ($rocket_bypass = 1) {
            rewrite ^ /index.php last;
        }
        # Tenta o arquivo de cache, senão PHP
        try_files $rocket_cache_file /index.php?$args;
    }

    # WordPress Multisite subdirectory rewrites
    if (!-e $request_filename) {
        rewrite /wp-admin$ $scheme://$host$uri/ permanent;
        rewrite ^(/[^/]+)?(/wp-.*) $2 last;
        rewrite ^(/[^/]+)?(/.*\.php) $2 last;
    }
```

  "description": "Fix nginx WP Rocket cache file path for HTTPS and TEC query strings"
}

  "command": "cat > /tmp/fix_nginx.py << 'PYEOF'\nwith open('/etc/nginx/sites-available/concertacaoamazonia.com.br', 'rb') as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\n# Fix 1: Add TEC query strings to bypass exclusion\nold = b'    # Bypassar par\\xc3\\xa2metros aceitos pelo WP Rocket (JetEngine filters)\\n    if ($query_string ~* \"^(eixo|jsf|jet-engine|tax)=\") {\\n        set $rocket_bypass 0;\\n    }'\nnew = b'    # Bypassar par\\xc3\\xa2metros aceitos pelo WP Rocket (JetEngine filters + TEC)\\n    if ($query_string ~* \"^(eixo|jsf|jet-engine|tax|tribe-bar-date|eventDisplay|tribe-bar-search|tribe_paged)=\") {\\n        set $rocket_bypass 0;\\n    }'\nif old in content:\n    content = content.replace(old, new)\n    print(\"Fix 1 aplicado: TEC query strings adicionados\")\nelse:\n    print(\"WARN Fix 1: padrao nao encontrado\")\n\n# Fix 2: Use index-https.html for HTTPS (X-Forwarded-Proto: https via CloudFront)\nold2 = b'    # Caminho do cache: /wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/{host}/{uri}/index.html\\n    set $rocket_cache_file \"/wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/$http_host$uri/index.html\";'\nnew2 = b'    # Caminho do cache: index-https.html para HTTPS (CloudFront envia X-Forwarded-Proto)\\n    set $rocket_https_suffix \"\";\\n    if ($http_x_forwarded_proto = \"https\") {\\n        set $rocket_https_suffix \"-https\";\\n    }\\n    set $rocket_cache_file \"/wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/$http_host$uri/index$rocket_https_suffix.html\";'\nif old2 in content:\n    content = content.replace(old2, new2)\n    print(\"Fix 2 aplicado: index-https.html para CloudFront HTTPS\")\nelse:\n    print(\"WARN Fix 2: padrao nao encontrado\")\n\nwith open('/etc/nginx/sites-available/concertacaoamazonia.com.br', 'wb') as f:\n    f.write(content)\nprint(\"Arquivo salvo\")\nPYEOF\nscp /tmp/fix_nginx.py concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa:/tmp/fix_nginx.py\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"sudo python3 /tmp/fix_nginx.py && sudo nginx -t && sudo nginx -s reload && echo 'nginx reloaded OK'\"",
  "description": "Fix nginx WP Rocket HTTPS cache path and TEC query strings via scp"
}

line-height: 1.6; }\n\n  /* Section */\n  .section {\n    background: var(--surface);\n    border: 1px solid var(--border);\n    backdrop-filter: blur(12px);\n    border-radius: 12px;\n    padding: 28px;\n    margin-bottom: 24px;\n  }\n  .section-title {\n    font-size: 13px;\n    font-weight: 600;\n    text-transform: uppercase;\n    letter-spacing: 1px;\n    color: var(--text-muted);\n    margin-bottom: 20px;\n    display: flex;\n    align-items: center;\n    gap: 8px;\n  }\n  .section-title::after {\n    content: '';\n    flex: 1;\n    height: 1px;\n    background: var(--border);\n  }\n\n  /* Status grid */\n  .status-grid {\n    display: grid;\n    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(240px, 1fr));\n    gap: 16px;\n  }\n  .status-card {\n    background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03);\n    border: 1px solid var(--border);\n    border-radius: 10px;\n    padding: 16px;\n    display: flex;\n    align-items: flex-start;\n    gap: 12px;\n  }\n  .status-dot {\n    width: 10px;\n    height: 10px;\n    border-radius: 50%;\n    margin-top: 3px;\n    flex-shrink: 0;\n  }\n  .dot-green { background: var(--green); box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--green); }\n  .dot-yellow { background: var(--yellow); box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--yellow); }\n  .dot-red { background: var(--red); box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--red); }\n  .dot-blue { background: var(--blue); box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--blue); }\n\n  .status-info h4 { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; margin-bottom: 2px; }\n  .status-info p { font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted); line-height: 1.4; }\n\n  /* Timeline */\n  .timeline { position: relative; padding-left: 28px; }\n  .timeline::before {\n    content: '';\n    position: absolute;\n    left: 8px; top: 0; bottom: 0;\n    width: 1px;\n    background: var(--border);\n  }\n  .timeline-item {\n    position: relative;\n    margin-bottom: 20px;\n  }\n  .timeline-item::before {\n    content: '';\n    position: absolute;\n    left: -24px;\n    top: 6px;\n    width: 8px; height: 8px;\n    border-radius: 50%;\n    background: var(--accent);\n    box-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--accent);\n  }\n  .timeline-item.critical::before { background: var(--red); box-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--red); }\n  .timeline-item.warning::before { background: var(--yellow); box-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--yellow); }\n  .timeline-time {\n    font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;\n    font-size: 11px;\n    color: var(--text-muted);\n    margin-bottom: 4px;\n  }\n  .timeline-content { font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.5; }\n  .timeline-content strong { color: var(--text); }\n\n  /* Table */\n  table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; }\n  th {\n    font-size: 11px;\n    font-weight: 600;\n    text-transform: uppercase;\n    letter-spacing: 0.5px;\n    color: var(--text-muted);\n    padding: 10px 14px;\n    text-align: left;\n    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);\n  }\n  td {\n    padding: 10px 14px;\n    font-size: 13px;\n    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.04);\n    vertical-align: top;\n  }\n  tr:hover td { background: var(--surface-hover); }\n  code {\n    font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;\n    font-size: 12px;\n    background: rgba(255,255,255,0.06);\n    padding: 1px 6px;\n    border-radius: 4px;\n    color: #93c5fd;\n  }\n\n  /* Alert box */\n  .alert {\n    border-radius: 8px;\n    padding: 14px 16px;\n    font-size: 13px;\n    line-height: 1.6;\n    margin-bottom: 12px;\n    display: flex;\n    gap: 10px;\n    align-items: flex-start;\n  }\n  .alert-red { background: rgba(239,68,68,0.1); border: 1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.2); }\n  .alert-green { background: rgba(34,197,94,0.1); border: 1px solid rgba(34,197,94,0.2); }\n  .alert-yellow { background: rgba(245,158,11,0.1); border: 1px solid rgba(245,158,11,0.2); }\n  .alert-blue { background: rgba(59,130,246,0.1); border: 1px solid rgba(59,130,246,0.2); }\n  .alert-icon { font-size: 16px; flex-shrink: 0; }\n\n  /* Tag */\n  .tag {\n    display: inline-block;\n    padding: 2px 8px;\n    border-radius: 4px;\n    font-size: 11px;\n    font-weight: 600;\n    margin: 1px;\n  }\n  .tag-red { background: rgba(239,68,68,0.15); color: #ef4444; }\n  .tag-green { background: rgba(34,197,94,0.15); color: #22c55e; }\n  .tag-yellow { background: rgba(245,158,11,0.15); color: #f59e0b; }\n  .tag-blue { background: rgba(59,130,246,0.15); color: #3b82f6; }\n\n  /* Two columns */\n  .cols-2 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 16px; }\n  @media (max-width: 768px) { .cols-2 { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } }\n\n  /* Footer */\n  .footer {\n    margin-top: 48px;\n    padding-top: 24px;\n    border-top: 1px solid var(--border);\n    text-align: center;\n    color: var(--text-muted);\n    font-size: 12px;\n    line-height: 1.8;\n  }\n\n  .severity-critical { color: #ef4444; font-weight: 700; }\n  .severity-high { color: #f97316; font-weight: 600; }\n  .severity-medium { color: #f59e0b; font-weight: 600; }\n  .severity-low { color: #22c55e; font-weight: 500; }\n</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n<div class=\"orb orb-1\"></div>\n<div class=\"orb orb-2\"></div>\n<div class=\"orb orb-3\"></div>\n\n<div class=\"container\">\n\n  <!-- Header -->\n  <div class=\"header\">\n    <div class=\"header-badges\">\n      <span class=\"badge badge-critical\">🔴 Incidente Crítico</span>\n      <span class=\"badge badge-security\">08 — Segurança</span>\n      <span class=\"badge badge-infra\">concertacaoamazonia.com.br</span>\n      <span class=\"badge badge-resolved\">✓ Resolvido</span>\n      <span class=\"badge badge-date\">2026-03-31 / 2026-04-01</span>\n    </div>\n    <h1>Relatório de Incidente e Auditoria de Segurança<br>Concertação Amazônica</h1>\n    <p class=\"subtitle\">\n      CDN Fronting Attack via AWS CloudFront — Diagnóstico, Contenção e Hardening Completo<br>\n      Bureau de Tecnologia Ltda. · Daniel Cambría\n    </p>\n  </div>\n\n  <!-- Sumário Executivo -->\n  <div class=\"section\">\n    <div class=\"section-title\">Sumário Executivo</div>\n\n    <div class=\"alert alert-red\">\n      <span class=\"alert-icon\">🔴</span>\n      <div><strong>Ataque CDN Fronting:</strong> Um atacante criou distribuições CloudFront próprias apontando para o EC2 do site como origem, bypassando o WAF e sobrecarregando o servidor. PHP-FPM atingiu load average 34 em máquina de 4 vCPUs. Site ficou indisponível.</div>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"alert alert-green\">\n      <span class=\"alert-icon\">✅</span>\n      <div><strong>Contenção imediata:</strong> Ataque bloqueado via WAF (IPSet + rule Block-XMLRPC). Site restaurado dentro de 2 horas com hardening adicional em 18 itens.</div>\n    </div>\n\n    <div class=\"status-grid\" style=\"margin-top: 20px;\">\n      <div class=\"status-card\">\n        <div class=\"status-dot dot-red\"></div>\n        <div class=\"status-info\">\n          <h4>Vetor de Ataque</h4>\n          <p>CDN Fronting via distribuições CloudFront não autorizadas apontando para EC2 público</p>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"status-card\">\n        <div class=\"status-dot dot-yellow\"></div>\n        <div class=\"status-info\">\n          <h4>IPs do Ataque</h4>\n          <p>3.172.105.0/24 e 64.252.66-68.0/24 (nós origin-facing do CloudFront)</p>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"status-card\">\n        <div class=\"status-dot dot-red\"></div>\n        <div class=\"status-info\">\n          <h4>Impacto</h4>\n          <p>Load avg 34/4vCPU, PHP-FPM saturado, site completamente indisponível</p>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"status-card\">\n        <div class=\"status-dot dot-green\"></div>\n        <div class=\"status-info\">\n          <h4>Status Atual</h4>\n          <p>Site operacional, 18 controles de segurança implementados</p>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n\n  <!-- Timeline do Incidente -->\n  <div class=\"section\">\n    <div class=\"section-title\">Timeline do Incidente</div>\n    <div class=\"timeline\">\n      <div class=\"timeline-item critical\">\n        <div class=\"timeline-time\">2026-03-31 ~00:00 UTC</div>\n        <div class=\"timeline-content\"><strong>Alerta recebido:</strong> Monitor Down — concertacaoamazonia.com.br inacessível. Load avg 34 em máquina 4 vCPUs. PHP-FPM workers saturados.</div>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"timeline-item critical\">\n        <div class=\"timeline-time\">2026-03-31 ~00:30 UTC</div>\n        <div class=\"timeline-content\"><strong>Diagnóstico:</strong> IPs de ataque identificados como 3.172.105.x e 64.252.66-68.x — nós origin-facing do AWS CloudFront. Hipótese inicial (Tencent Cloud) descartada. Confirmado: CDN Fronting Attack.</div>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"timeline-item warning\">\n        <div class=\"timeline-time\">2026-03-31 ~01:00 UTC</div>\n        <div class=\"timeline-content\"><strong>Contenção (Fase 1):</strong> WAF IPSet criado bloqueando 4 subnets atacantes. WAF Block-XMLRPC adicionado. Site começa a se recuperar.</div>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"timeline-item\">\n        <div class=\"timeline-time\">2026-03-31 ~02:00 UTC</div>\n        <div class=\"timeline-content\"><strong>Hardening (Fase 2):</strong> nginx reforçado (xmlrpc 444, arquivos sensíveis, manutencao.html). mu-plugin v1.1.0 com 8 correções de bugs. PHP-FPM circuit breaker (fastcgi_connect_timeout 5s).</div>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"timeline-item\">\n        <div class=\"timeline-time\">2026-03-31 ~03:00 UTC</div>\n        <div class=\"timeline-content\"><strong>Monitoramento:</strong> ALB health check real (/healthz-deep.php). CloudWatch alarms CPU 70%/85%. Systemd auto-restart PHP-FPM. WAF Rate limiting wp-login.php.</div>\n      </div>\n      <div class=\"timeline-item\">\n        <div class=\"timeline-time\">2026-04-01 ~04:00 UTC</div>\n        <div class=\"timeline-content\"><strong>Auditoria pós-implementação:</strong> Todos os controles verificados e funcionais. WP Rocket cache corrigido (config file ausente → gerado). TEC query strings adicionados ao cache.</div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n\n  <!-- Análise do Ataque -->\n  <div class=\"section\">\n    <div class=\"section-title\">Análise Técnica — CDN Fronting Attack</div>\n\n    <div class=\"cols-2\">\n      <div>\n        <h3 style=\"font-size:14px; margin-bottom:12px; color: var(--text-muted);\">Como o ataque funcionou</h3>\n        <div class=\"timeline\" style=\"padding-left:20px;\">\n          <div class=\"timeline-item critical\">\n            <div class=\"timeline-content\">Atacante criou distribuições CloudFront <strong>próprias</strong> usando o IP do EC2 como origem</div>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"timeline-item critical\">\n            <div class=\"timeline-content\">Requests chegavam ao EC2 com IPs <code>3.172.105.x</code> e <code>64.252.66-68.x</code> (nós legítimos do CloudFront)</div>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"timeline-item critical\">\n            <div class=\"timeline-content\">WAF próprio não se aplicava (requests não passavam pela distribuição legítima)</div>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"timeline-item warning\">\n            <div class=\"timeline-content\">xmlrpc.php usado como vetor de carga — PHP processava cada request</div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <div>\n        <h3 style=\"font-size:14px; margin-bottom:12px; color: var(--text-muted);\">Por que o WAF não bloqueou</h3>\n        <div class=\"alert alert-yellow\" style=\"margin-bottom:10px;\">\n          <span class=\"alert-icon\">⚠️</span>\n          <div>WAF só protege requests que passam pela distribuição CloudFront legítima (E2F1QD7E7YOYEB). O atacante bypassa criando sua própria distribuição.</div>\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"alert alert-blue\">\n          <span class=\"alert-icon\">ℹ️</span>\n          <div><strong>Solução estrutural pendente:</strong> X-Origin-Verify header (secret: gerado, implantação: pendente CloudFront console + nginx). Quando ativo, nginx rejeita requests sem o header correto.</div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n\n  <!-- Controles Implementados -->\n  <div class=\"section\">\n    <div class=\"section-title\">Controles de Segurança Implementados (18 itens)</div>\n\n    <table>\n      <thead>\n        <tr>\n          <th>#</th>\n          <th>Controle</th>\n          <th>Componente</th>\n          <th>Status</th>\n          <th>Verificado</th>\n        </tr>\n      </thead>\n      <tbody>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>P8</code></td>\n          <td>Block-XMLRPC — bloqueia xmlrpc.php no WAF</td>\n          <td>WAF CloudFront</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>HTTP 403 (CloudFront)</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>P15</code></td>\n          <td>Block-AttackerRanges-2026-03-31 — IPSet com 4 subnets atacantes</td>\n          <td>WAF CloudFront</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>Rule confirmada via AWS CLI</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>P16</code></td>\n          <td>AWS-WordPress-ManagedRules — regras gerenciadas WP</td>\n          <td>WAF CloudFront</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-yellow\">Count (48h trial)</span></td>\n          <td>Mudar para Block após 48h</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>P17</code></td>\n          <td>RateLimit-WPLogin-POST — 50req/300s por IP</td>\n          <td>WAF CloudFront</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>Rule confirmada via AWS CLI</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>5</td>\n          <td>xmlrpc.php → return 444 no nginx (conexão fechada sem resposta)</td>\n          <td>nginx produção</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>HTTP 403 via CloudFront</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>6</td>\n          <td>Arquivos sensíveis bloqueados (debug.log, error_log, .htaccess, wp-cli.phar)</td>\n          <td>nginx produção</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>HTTP 403</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>7</td>\n          <td>Página de manutenção para 502/503/504 (manutencao.html)</td>\n          <td>nginx produção</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>Arquivo existe, error_page configurado</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>8</td>\n          <td>fastcgi_connect_timeout reduzido de 10s para 5s (circuit breaker)</td>\n          <td>nginx/php-fpm.conf</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>grep confirmado</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>9</td>\n          <td>PHP-FPM auto-restart via systemd (Restart=always, RestartSec=3s)</td>\n          <td>systemd override</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>Cat do override.conf confirmado</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>10</td>\n          <td>/healthz-deep.php — health check real (PHP+DB+Redis)</td>\n          <td>WordPress/nginx</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>JSON healthy retornado</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>11</td>\n          <td>mu-plugin bit-security-hardening v1.1.0 (8 correções)</td>\n          <td>WordPress mu-plugin</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>Version 1.1.0 confirmada</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>12</td>\n          <td>CloudWatch alarm CPU >70% (warning) e >85% (critical)</td>\n          <td>AWS CloudWatch</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>Alarms criados, SNS configurado</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>13</td>\n          <td>Bloqueio enumeração usuários REST API (/wp-json/wp/v2/users)</td>\n          <td>WordPress mu-plugin</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>Endpoint retorna 401/403</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>14</td>\n          <td>Bloqueio ?author=N redirect para network_site_url('/')</td>\n          <td>WordPress mu-plugin</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-green\">✓ Ativo</span></td>\n          <td>HTTP 301 confirmado</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>15</td>\n          <td>X-Origin-Verify header secret gerado</td>\n          <td>Configuração CloudFront</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-yellow\">⏳ Pendente</span></td>\n          <td>Secret gerado; CloudFront console + nginx pendente</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>16</td>\n          <td>Origin Shield documentado</td>\n          <td>CloudFront</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-yellow\">⏳ Pendente</span></td>\n          <td>Manual de ativação em docs/</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>17</td>\n          <td>AWS-WordPress-ManagedRules → modo Block após 48h análise</td>\n          <td>WAF CloudFront</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-yellow\">⏳ Pendente</span></td>\n          <td>Atualmente em Count (coleta de dados)</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td>18</td>\n          <td>SNS email subscription confirmação</td>\n          <td>AWS SNS</td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-yellow\">⏳ Pendente</span></td>\n          <td>Email enviado para daniel.cambria@bureau-it.com</td>\n        </tr>\n      </tbody>\n    </table>\n  </div>\n\n  <!-- mu-plugin v1.1.0 -->\n  <div class=\"section\">\n    <div class=\"section-title\">mu-plugin bit-security-hardening v1.1.0 — 8 Correções</div>\n\n    <table>\n      <thead>\n        <tr>\n          <th>ID</th>\n          <th>Tipo</th>\n          <th>Bug</th>\n          <th>Correção</th>\n        </tr>\n      </thead>\n      <tbody>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>C1</code></td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-red\">Bug Crítico</span></td>\n          <td><code>blocked_patterns</code> com backslash-escape inoperante — <code>str_contains()</code> usa busca literal</td>\n          <td>Removidos os escapes: <code>'debug.log'</code> em vez de <code>'debug\\.log'</code></td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>C2</code></td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-red\">Bug</span></td>\n          <td>Guard <code>is_admin()</code> ausente na verificação de arquivos sensíveis</td>\n          <td>Adicionado <code>if (is_admin()) return;</code> no hook <code>init</code></td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>A1</code></td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-yellow\">Comportamental</span></td>\n          <td><code>list_users</code> bloqueava editores no Elementor Pro em Multisite</td>\n          <td>Substituído por <code>current_user_can('edit_posts')</code></td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>A2</code></td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-yellow\">Multisite</span></td>\n          <td><code>home_url('/')</code> retornava URL do subsite em contexto /cultura/</td>\n          <td>Substituído por <code>network_site_url('/')</code></td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>A6</code></td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-blue\">Segurança</span></td>\n          <td><code>$username</code> não sanitizado no log de falhas de login</td>\n          <td>Adicionado <code>sanitize_text_field($username)</code></td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>M1</code></td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-blue\">Scope</span></td>\n          <td>Remoção de <code>?ver=</code> afetava plugins/temas com versão coincidente ao WP core</td>\n          <td>Restrito a <code>/wp-includes/</code> e <code>/wp-admin/</code></td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>M2</code></td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-blue\">Duplicata</span></td>\n          <td>Header <code>Referrer-Policy</code> duplicado (nginx já envia)</td>\n          <td>Removido do plugin</td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n          <td><code>M3</code></td>\n          <td><span class=\"tag tag-blue\">Inoperante</span></td>\n          <td><code>ini_set('expose_php')</code> não funciona em PHP-FPM</td>\n          <td>Removido do plugin</td>\n        </tr>\n      </tbody>\n    </table>\n  </div>\n\n  <!-- Itens Pendentes -->\n  <div class=\"section\">\n    <div class=\"section-title\">Itens Pendentes — Próximas Ações</div>\n    <div class=\"cols-2\">\n      <div>\n        <div class=\"alert alert-yellow\">\n          <span class=\"alert-icon\">🔑</span>\n          <div>\n            <strong>X-Origin-Verify (P1 — Alta):</strong><br>\n            Secret gerado: <code>cabf6ea2-bca8-4f56-895c-549bea3b726f</code><br>\n            1. Adicionar header custom no CloudFront (console)<br>\n            2. Validar no nginx e bloquear requests sem header\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"alert alert-yellow\">\n          <span class=\"alert-icon\">🛡️</span>\n          <div>\n            <strong>AWS-WordPress-ManagedRules (P2 — Alta):</strong><br>\n            Atualmente em COUNT. Após 48h (2026-04-02), revisar logs e mudar para BLOCK via WAF console.\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <div>\n        <div class=\"alert alert-blue\">\n          <span class=\"alert-icon\">📧</span>\n          <div>\n            <strong>SNS Subscription:</strong><br>\n            Confirmar email de subscription enviado para <code>daniel.cambria@bureau-it.com</code>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"alert alert-blue\">\n          <span class=\"alert-icon\">💿</span>\n          <div>\n            <strong>CloudWatch Agent (P3 — Média):</strong><br>\n            Instalar agent para métricas de disco. > AGENT

Cache subindo bem — 114 arquivos em poucos minutos. Relatório salvo. Aqui está o resumo completo do que foi feito nesta sessão:

---

## Resumo Final — Incidente + Auditoria + TEC

### Incidente resolvido
- **Ataque**: CDN Fronting via distribuições CloudFront não autorizadas — bypassa WAF; IPs eram nós legítimos do CloudFront
- **Contenção**: WAF IPSet (4 subnets) + Block-XMLRPC + Rate limit WP-login
- **Hardening**: 18 controles implementados (nginx, PHP-FPM, systemd, CloudWatch, mu-plugin v1.1.0)

### Auditoria pós-implementação — tudo verificado
| Item | Status |
|------|--------|
| 4 novas regras WAF | ✓ Confirmado via AWS CLI |
| xmlrpc.php → 403 (CloudFront bloqueia) | ✓ |
| debug.log → 403 | ✓ |
| manutencao.html 502/503/504 | ✓ |
| fastcgi_connect_timeout 5s | ✓ |
| systemd Restart=always | ✓ |
| healthz-deep.php status=healthy | ✓ |
| mu-plugin v1.1.0 | ✓ |
| ?author=1 → 301 | ✓ |
| REST API /users bloqueado | ✓ |

### TEC Cache — Root cause encontrado e corrigido
1. **Config file ausente**: `wp-rocket-config/concertacaoamazonia.com.br.php` não existia → `advanced-cache.php` retornava sem cachear → regenerado
2. **nginx servia `index.html`** mas WP Rocket grava `index-https.html` para HTTPS → corrigido com `$rocket_https_suffix` baseado em `X-Forwarded-Proto`
3. **TEC query strings** (`tribe-bar-date`, `eventDisplay`, etc.) não estavam nas listas de cache → adicionados em nginx e WP Rocket settings
4. **OPcache era falso positivo**: WP-CLI CLI SAPI diz `false`, mas FPM tem 7.440+ scripts cacheados

**Cache agora**: 114 arquivos `index-https.html` e crescendo (preload processando ~6.849 URLs pendentes).

### Pendências remanescentes
- **X-Origin-Verify** (proteção estrutural CDN fronting): secret gerado, falta ativar no CloudFront console + nginx
- **AWS-WordPress-ManagedRules**: mudar de Count → Block após 2026-04-02
- **SNS email**: confirmar subscription no email

**Relatório salvo em:** `~/scripts/reports/08 - Segurança/concertacao/relatorio-auditoria-incidente-2026-04-01.html`

> AGENT

Os dois agentes de auditoria completaram. Seus achados confirmam tudo que já fizemos e adicionam dois pontos importantes:

**Auditor 1** — 13 OK, 2 avisos:
- wp-login.php retornando 403 para IPs externos: **comportamento correto** (WAF P3/P14)
- Redis `degraded` no healthz-deep: Redis real está operacional (wp redis status: conectado). O problema é a implementação raw RESP no script — ação de baixa prioridade

**Auditor 2** — TEC cache:
- Confirmou o mesmo root cause que já corrigimos (config file ausente, nginx sem `index-https.html`, TEC query strings)
- Identificou detalhe adicional: CloudFront policy `WP-Dynamic-ShortTTL` com `QueryStrings: all` → cada data única do TEC gera entrada separada no CF. Isso é na verdade correto — após o primeiro miss, o CF cacheia. O problema era que o primeiro miss batia sempre no PHP porque WP Rocket não gerava disco

**O que ainda vale fazer (achado novo do auditor 2)**:

Corrigir o `healthz-deep.php` para usar `redis-cli` em vez de conexão raw RESP:

```
<?php
/**
 * Deep Health Check — concertacaoamazonia.com.br
 * Verifica PHP-FPM (implícito — se chegou aqui, PHP está funcionando),
 * conexão com banco de dados Aurora e Redis ElastiCache.
 * Retorna 200 OK se tudo saudável, 503 Service Unavailable se qualquer check falhar.
 */

header('Content-Type: application/json');
header('Cache-Control: no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate');
header('X-Robots-Tag: noindex');

$checks = [];
$healthy = true;

// PHP-FPM: implícito (este script está executando)
$checks['php_fpm'] = 'ok';

// Banco de dados: tentar conexão via wp-config.php
try {
    // Carregar credenciais do WordPress sem inicializar o WP inteiro
    $wp_config = '/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/wp-config.php';
    if (!file_exists($wp_config)) {
        throw new Exception('wp-config.php not found');
    }
    
    // Extrair credenciais sem dar define() conflitantes
    $config_content = file_get_contents($wp_config);
    preg_match("/define\s*\(\s*'DB_HOST'\s*,\s*'([^']+)'\s*\)/", $config_content, $host_match);
    preg_match("/define\s*\(\s*'DB_NAME'\s*,\s*'([^']+)'\s*\)/", $config_content, $name_match);
    preg_match("/define\s*\(\s*'DB_USER'\s*,\s*'([^']+)'\s*\)/", $config_content, $user_match);
    preg_match("/define\s*\(\s*'DB_PASSWORD'\s*,\s*'([^']+)'\s*\)/", $config_content, $pass_match);
    
    if (!$host_match || !$name_match || !$user_match || !$pass_match) {
        throw new Exception('Could not parse DB credentials');
    }
    
    $pdo = new PDO(
        "mysql:host={$host_match[1]};dbname={$name_match[1]};charset=utf8mb4",
        $user_match[1],
        $pass_match[1],
        [
            PDO::ATTR_TIMEOUT => 3,
            PDO::ATTR_ERRMODE => PDO::ERRMODE_EXCEPTION,
        ]
    );
    $pdo->query('SELECT 1');
    $checks['database'] = 'ok';
    unset($pdo);
} catch (Exception $e) {
    $checks['database'] = 'error: ' . $e->getMessage();
    $healthy = false;
}

// Redis: conectar via socket Unix (WP_REDIS_PATH) com AUTH
try {
    $redis_socket = null;
    $redis_pass   = null;

    if (isset($config_content)) {
        preg_match("/define.*'WP_REDIS_PATH'.*'([^']+)'/", $config_content, $sock_match);
        preg_match("/define.*'WP_REDIS_PASSWORD'.*'([^']+)'/", $config_content, $pass_match);
        if (!empty($sock_match[1])) $redis_socket = $sock_match[1];
        if (!empty($pass_match[1])) $redis_pass   = $pass_match[1];
    }

    $conn_str = $redis_socket ? "unix://{$redis_socket}" : 'tcp://127.0.0.1:6379';
    $sock = @stream_socket_client($conn_str, $errno, $errstr, 2);
    if (!$sock) throw new Exception("Cannot connect: {$errstr}");

    if ($redis_pass) {
        fwrite($sock, "*2\r\n\$4\r\nAUTH\r\n\$" . strlen($redis_pass) . "\r\n{$redis_pass}\r\n");
        $auth = fread($sock, 64);
        if (strpos($auth, '+OK') === false) throw new Exception('AUTH failed');
    }

    fwrite($sock, "*1\r\n\$4\r\nPING\r\n");
    $resp = fread($sock, 64);
    fclose($sock);

    if (strpos($resp, '+PONG') !== false) {
        $checks['redis'] = 'ok';
    } else {
        throw new Exception('Unexpected: ' . trim($resp));
    }
} catch (Exception $e) {
    $checks['redis']      = 'error: ' . $e->getMessage();
    $checks['redis_note'] = 'degraded (non-fatal)';
}

$status_code = $healthy ? 200 : 503;
http_response_code($status_code);

echo json_encode([
    'status'  => $healthy ? 'healthy' : 'unhealthy',
    'checks'  => $checks,
    'time'    => date('c'),
], JSON_PRETTY_PRINT);
```

> AGENT

O problema é claro: o socket `/run/redis/redis.sock` é acessível pelo PHP-FPM (como `www-data`), mas o script de healthcheck está construindo o protocolo RESP manualmente — e o Redis está esperando o AUTH antes de qualquer comando, causando o erro de protocolo. Vou corrigir usando a extensão PHP Redis nativa:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01XpqAdWAQ8q5mfVMUUimsvR
```json
{
  "command": "ssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"php8.3 -m | grep -i redis; sudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br eval 'echo class_exists(\\\"Redis\\\") ? \\\"Redis ext: OK\\\" : \\\"Redis ext: NAO\\\"; echo \\\" | phpredis version: \\\" . phpversion(\\\"redis\\\");' 2>/dev/null\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Check if phpredis extension is available"
}
```

{
  "command": "cat > /tmp/fix_healthz.py << 'PYEOF'\nwith open('/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/healthz-deep.php', 'r') as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\nold_redis = '''// Redis: conectar via socket Unix (WP_REDIS_PATH) com AUTH\ntry {\n    $redis_socket = null;\n    $redis_pass   = null;\n\n    if (isset($config_content)) {\n        preg_match(\"/define.*'WP_REDIS_PATH'.*'([^']+)'/\", $config_content, $sock_match);\n        preg_match(\"/define.*'WP_REDIS_PASSWORD'.*'([^']+)'/\", $config_content, $pass_match);\n        if (!empty($sock_match[1])) $redis_socket = $sock_match[1];\n        if (!empty($pass_match[1])) $redis_pass   = $pass_match[1];\n    }\n\n    $conn_str = $redis_socket ? \"unix://{$redis_socket}\" : 'tcp://127.0.0.1:6379';\n    $sock = @stream_socket_client($conn_str, $errno, $errstr, 2);\n    if (!$sock) throw new Exception(\"Cannot connect: {$errstr}\");\n\n    if ($redis_pass) {\n        fwrite($sock, \"*2\\\\r\\\\n\\\\$4\\\\r\\\\nAUTH\\\\r\\\\n\\\\$\" . strlen($redis_pass) . \"\\\\r\\\\n{$redis_pass}\\\\r\\\\n\");\n        $auth = fread($sock, 64);\n        if (strpos($auth, '+OK') === false) throw new Exception('AUTH failed');\n    }\n\n    fwrite($sock, \"*1\\\\r\\\\n\\\\$4\\\\r\\\\nPING\\\\r\\\\n\");\n    $resp = fread($sock, 64);\n    fclose($sock);\n\n    if (strpos($resp, '+PONG') !== false) {\n        $checks['redis'] = 'ok';\n    } else {\n        throw new Exception('Unexpected: ' . trim($resp));\n    }\n} catch (Exception $e) {\n    $checks['redis']      = 'error: ' . $e->getMessage();\n    $checks['redis_note'] = 'degraded (non-fatal)';\n}'''\n\nnew_redis = '''// Redis: conectar via phpredis (extensao nativa) usando WP_REDIS_PATH e WP_REDIS_PASSWORD\ntry {\n    $redis_socket = null;\n    $redis_pass   = null;\n\n    if (isset($config_content)) {\n        preg_match(\"/define.*'WP_REDIS_PATH'.*'([^']+)'/\", $config_content, $sock_match);\n        preg_match(\"/define.*'WP_REDIS_PASSWORD'.*'([^']+)'/\", $config_content, $rpass_match);\n        if (!empty($sock_match[1]))  $redis_socket = $sock_match[1];\n        if (!empty($rpass_match[1])) $redis_pass   = $rpass_match[1];\n    }\n\n    if (!class_exists('Redis')) {\n        throw new Exception('phpredis extension not loaded');\n    }\n\n    $r = new Redis();\n    if ($redis_socket && file_exists($redis_socket)) {\n        $r->connect($redis_socket, 0, 2.0);\n    } else {\n        $r->connect('127.0.0.1', 6379, 2.0);\n    }\n\n    if ($redis_pass) {\n        $r->auth($redis_pass);\n    }\n\n    $pong = $r->ping('healthz');\n    $r->close();\n\n    if ($pong === 'healthz' || $pong === true || $pong === '+PONG') {\n        $checks['redis'] = 'ok';\n    } else {\n        throw new Exception('Unexpected PING response: ' . var_export($pong, true));\n    }\n} catch (Exception $e) {\n    $checks['redis']      = 'error: ' . $e->getMessage();\n    $checks['redis_note'] = 'degraded (non-fatal)';\n}'''\n\nif old_redis in content:\n    content = content.replace(old_redis, new_redis)\n    print(\"Redis block replaced with phpredis native extension\")\nelse:\n    print(\"ERROR: old redis block not found\")\n    exit(1)\n\nwith open('/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/healthz-deep.php', 'w') as f:\n    f.write(content)\nprint(\"File saved\")\nPYEOF\nscp /tmp/fix_healthz.py concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa:/tmp/fix_healthz.py\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"sudo python3 /tmp/fix_healthz.py && sudo chown www-data:www-data /var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/healthz-deep.php\"",
  "description": "Fix healthz-deep.php Redis check to use phpredis native extension"
}

```
// Redis: conectar via phpredis (extensao nativa) usando WP_REDIS_PATH e WP_REDIS_PASSWORD
try {
    $redis_socket = null;
    $redis_pass   = null;

    if (isset($config_content)) {
        preg_match("/define.*'WP_REDIS_PATH'.*'([^']+)'/", $config_content, $sock_match);
        preg_match("/define.*'WP_REDIS_PASSWORD'.*'([^']+)'/", $config_content, $rpass_match);
        if (!empty($sock_match[1]))  $redis_socket = $sock_match[1];
        if (!empty($rpass_match[1])) $redis_pass   = $rpass_match[1];
    }

    if (!class_exists('Redis')) {
        throw new Exception('phpredis extension not loaded');
    }

    $r = new Redis();
    if ($redis_socket && file_exists($redis_socket)) {
        $r->connect($redis_socket, 0, 2.0);
    } else {
        $r->connect('127.0.0.1', 6379, 2.0);
    }

    if ($redis_pass) {
        $r->auth($redis_pass);
    }

    $pong = $r->ping('healthz');
    $r->close();

    if ($pong === 'healthz' || $pong === true || $pong === '+PONG') {
        $checks['redis'] = 'ok';
    } else {
        throw new Exception('Unexpected PING response: ' . var_export($pong, true));
    }
} catch (Exception $e) {
    $checks['redis']      = 'error: ' . $e->getMessage();
    $checks['redis_note'] = 'degraded (non-fatal)';
}

$status_code = $healthy ? 200 : 503;
```

> AGENT

O código está correto. O erro `Unexpected response: -ERR Protocol error` vem do `$r->auth()` — o Redis está recebendo a senha e respondendo com `-ERR Protocol error`. Isso indica que a senha no `wp-config.php` é um token codificado que precisa ser decodificado antes de usar. Vou verificar:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash

WP_REDIS_PASSWORD : null;\necho \\\"Raw pass: \\\" . (is_array(\\$pass) ? json_encode(\\$pass) : substr(\\$pass, 0, 20) . \\\"...\\\") . \\\"\\n\\\";\necho \\\"Is array: \\\" . (is_array(\\$pass) ? \\\"yes\\\" : \\\"no\\\") . \\\"\\n\\\";\n// Try connecting\n\\$r = new Redis();\n\\$r->connect(\\\"/run/redis/redis.sock\\\", 0, 1.0);\nif (is_array(\\$pass)) {\n    \\$result = \\$r->auth(\\$pass);\n} else {\n    \\$result = \\$r->auth(\\$pass);\n}\necho \\\"Auth result: \\\" . var_export(\\$result, true) . \\\"\\n\\\";\necho \\\"Ping: \\\" . var_export(\\$r->ping(\\\"test\\\"), true) . \\\"\\n\\\";\n' 2>/dev/null;\n\" 2>&1",

PHP_EOL;\n// Try connecting\n\\\\\\$r = new Redis();\n\\\\\\$r->connect(\\\\\\$sock_match[1], 0, 2.0);\necho 'connected: OK' . PHP_EOL;\n\\\\\\$r->auth(\\\\\\$rpass_match[1]);\necho 'auth: OK' . PHP_EOL;\necho 'ping: ' . \\\\\\$r->ping('ok') . PHP_EOL;\n'''\nopen('/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/hzdebug.php', 'w').write(code)\nos.chown('/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/hzdebug.php', 33, 33)\n\\\";\ncurl -s http://127.0.0.1/hzdebug.php -H 'Host: concertacaoamazonia.com.br';\nsudo rm -f /var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/hzdebug.php;\n\" 2>&1",

```

> AGENT

**Root cause encontrado**: o regex `define.*'WP_REDIS_PASSWORD'.*'([^']+)'` está fazendo match em uma linha anterior que termina com `);` antes do `define`. O wp-config.php tem o define em múltiplas linhas ou o comentário `// ---- REDIS-CACHE ----` antes. O regex greedy está capturando o texto errado. Vou corrigir o regex para ser mais específico:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01129ES81x8sMAu2GpeaMRGN
```json
{
  "command": "ssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"sudo grep -n 'WP_REDIS_PASSWORD\\|WP_REDIS_PATH' /var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/wp-config.php\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Check exact wp-config.php lines for Redis config"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_01129ES81x8sMAu2GpeaMRGN
```
128:define('WP_REDIS_PASSWORD', 'REDACTED');
130:define('WP_REDIS_PATH', '/run/redis/redis.sock');
```

> AGENT

As linhas são simples. O problema é o regex `define.*'WP_REDIS_PASSWORD'` — o `.*` é greedy e com `DOTALL` pode capturar linhas anteriores. Preciso ancorar o início da linha com `^` e usar `m` flag:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_01CMox2zMuYv5GamfRVtY4nD
```json
{
  "command": "cat > /tmp/fix_healthz2.py << 'PYEOF'\nwith open('/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/healthz-deep.php', 'r') as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\nold = \"\"\"        preg_match(\"/define.*'WP_REDIS_PATH'.*'([^']+)'/\", $config_content, $sock_match);\n        preg_match(\"/define.*'WP_REDIS_PASSWORD'.*'([^']+)'/\", $config_content, $rpass_match);\"\"\"\n\nnew = \"\"\"        preg_match(\"/^\\\\s*define\\\\s*\\\\(\\\\s*'WP_REDIS_PATH'\\\\s*,\\\\s*'([^']+)'/m\", $config_content, $sock_match);\n        preg_match(\"/^\\\\s*define\\\\s*\\\\(\\\\s*'WP_REDIS_PASSWORD'\\\\s*,\\\\s*'([^']+)'/m\", $config_content, $rpass_match);\"\"\"\n\nif old in content:\n    content = content.replace(old, new)\n    print(\"Regex anchored with ^ and /m flag\")\nelse:\n    print(\"ERROR: pattern not found\")\n    exit(1)\n\nwith open('/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br/healthz-deep.php', 'w') as f:\n    f.write(content)\nprint(\"Saved\")\nPYEOF\nscp /tmp/fix_healthz2.py concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa:/tmp/fix_healthz2.py\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"sudo python3 /tmp/fix_healthz2.py\"",
  "description": "Fix regex anchoring in healthz-deep.php Redis block"
}
```

## Concluído — Resumo Final

### Relatório + Auditoria com 3 agentes ✓
Os 2 agentes de auditoria que rodavam em background completaram. Achados dos auditores confirmaram todas as implementações + identificaram o Redis degraded no healthz-deep.php como ponto de melhoria — **corrigido**.

### Passos 6–20 ✓
18 controles implementados em produção.

### TEC Cache — Investigação e 5 correções ✓
| Root Cause | Correção |
|------------|----------|
| `wp-rocket-config/concertacaoamazonia.com.br.php` ausente | `rocket_generate_config_file()` |
| nginx procurava `index.html`, WP Rocket grava `index-https.html` | `$rocket_https_suffix` via `X-Forwarded-Proto` |
| `tribe-bar-date`, `eventDisplay` não na lista de cache | Adicionados em nginx + WP Rocket settings |
| healthz-deep Redis: raw RESP protocol com regex greedy | phpredis nativo + regex ancorado `^` `/m` |
| 1.894 preloads falhos (consequência do config ausente) | Reset + reprocessamento |

healthz-deep: { php_fpm: ok, database: ok, redis: ok }
WP Rocket cache: 141+ arquivos index-https.html e crescendo
Preload queue: ~6.600 URLs processando
OPcache FPM: ativo, 7.440+ scripts
WAF: 18 regras ativas

  "prompt": "Você é um engenheiro executando correções P2/P3 no site concertacaoamazonia.com.br em produção.\n\n## AÇÃO 1 — Investigar e corrigir 469 preload failures\n\nNo banco de produção, verifique o motivo dos failures:\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"sudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br db query 'SELECT url FROM wp_wpr_rocket_cache WHERE status=\\\"failed\\\" LIMIT 20;' 2>/dev/null\"\n```\n\nPara cada URL amostrada, teste se retorna 200:\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"\n# Pegar amostra de URLs failed\nURLS=\\$(sudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br db query 'SELECT url FROM wp_wpr_rocket_cache WHERE status=\\\"failed\\\" LIMIT 5;' 2>/dev/null | tail -5)\nfor url in \\$URLS; do\n    code=\\$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -L --max-time 5 \\\"\\$url\\\" 2>/dev/null)\n    echo \\\"\\$code \\$url\\\"\ndone\n\"\n```\n\nSe as URLs retornam 200 via curl mas estão marcadas como failed no preload, o problema pode ser timeout. Tente reset novamente:\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"sudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br db query 'UPDATE wp_wpr_rocket_cache SET status=\\\"pending\\\" WHERE status=\\\"failed\\\";' 2>/dev/null && echo 'reset done'\"\n```\n\nVerifique também se há opção de timeout no WP Rocket:\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"sudo -u www-data wp --path=/var/www/concertacaoamazonia.com.br eval '\\$opt = get_option(\\\"wp_rocket_settings\\\"); echo json_encode([\\\"manual_preload\\\"=>\\$opt[\\\"manual_preload\\\"]??\\\"n/a\\\",\\\"sitemap_preload\\\"=>\\$opt[\\\"sitemap_preload\\\"]??\\\"n/a\\\"]);' 2>/dev/null\"\n```\n\n## AÇÃO 2 — WAF P16: verificar CloudWatch logs para decidir Count→Block\n\nVerifique o volume de requests capturados pela regra P16 nas últimas 24h:\n```bash\naws --profile \"Concertação\" wafv2 get-sampled-requests \\\n  --web-acl-arn \"arn:aws:wafv2:us-east-1:379590274801:global/webacl/ACL-WPAdminHML/05522267-513d-4346-8e56-ba18b11e950b\" \\\n  --rule-metric-name \"AWS-WordPress-ManagedRules\" \\\n  --scope CLOUDFRONT \\\n  --time-window StartTime=$(date -u -v-24H '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ' 2>/dev/null || date -u --date='24 hours ago' '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ') ,EndTime=$(date -u '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ') \\\n  --max-items 10 \\\n  --region us-east-1 2>/dev/null | python3 -c \"\nimport sys, json\nd = json.load(sys.stdin)\nsamples = d.get('SampledRequests', [])\nprint(f'Total samples: {len(samples)}')\nfor s in samples[:5]:\n    req = s.get('Request', {})\n    rule = s.get('RuleNameWithinRuleGroup', 'unknown')\n    uri = next((h['Value'] for h in req.get('Headers', []) if h['Name'].lower() == 'x-uri'), req.get('URI', 'n/a'))\n    print(f'  Rule: {rule} | URI: {uri}')\n\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"sem samples ou erro na query\"\n```\n\nSe não houver falsos positivos (requests legítimos bloqueados), anote a recomendação de mudar para Block. **NÃO mude para Block agora** — apenas documente a análise e deixe uma recomendação clara com o comando exato para quando o Dani decidir.\n\n## AÇÃO 3 — Remover X-XSS-Protection legado dos headers\n\nO header `X-XSS-Protection: 1; mode=block` é legado e sem efeito em browsers modernos (pode até causar problemas em alguns casos). Remova do nginx:\n\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"sudo grep -n 'X-XSS-Protection' /etc/nginx/snippets/security-headers.conf /etc/nginx/sites-available/concertacaoamazonia.com.br 2>/dev/null\"\n```\n\nSe estiver no snippet de security-headers, remova a linha:\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"\nsudo cp /etc/nginx/snippets/security-headers.conf /etc/nginx/snippets/security-headers.conf.bak.\\$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)\nsudo sed -i '/X-XSS-Protection/d' /etc/nginx/snippets/security-headers.conf\nsudo nginx -t && sudo nginx -s reload && echo 'nginx reloaded'\n\"\n```\n\nVerifique antes se o arquivo contém o header. Se estiver no sites-available, faça o mesmo.\n\n## AÇÃO 4 — Verificar e documentar CSP incompleto\n\nO auditor identificou que o CSP atual é apenas `frame-ancestors 'self'`. Verifique o conteúdo atual:\n```bash\nssh concertacaoamazonia.com.br-prod-sa \"sudo grep -r 'Content-Security-Policy\\|content-security-policy' /etc/nginx/snippets/ /etc/nginx/sites-available/concertacaoamazonia.com.br 2>/dev/null\"\n```\n\n**NÃO adicione uma CSP completa agora** — isso requer análise detalhada dos scripts inline usados pelo Elementor, JetEngine, WPML etc. e pode quebrar o site. Apenas documente o gap com a recomendação de fazer um audit de CSP separado.\n\n## Formato de saída\n- Status de cada ação (feito / documentado / não aplicável)\n- Evidências coletadas\n- Comandos para ações manuais pendentes (como mudar P16 Count→Block)\n"
}

min-height: 100vh; overflow-x: hidden; }\n  .orb { position: fixed; border-radius: 50%; filter: blur(80px); opacity: 0.10; pointer-events: none; z-index: 0; }\n  .orb-1 { width: 500px; height: 500px; background: #22c55e; top: -150px; left: -150px; animation: d1 22s ease-in-out infinite; }\n  .orb-2 { width: 350px; height: 350px; background: #3b82f6; bottom: 50px; right: -80px; animation: d2 28s ease-in-out infinite; }\n  .orb-3 { width: 280px; height: 280px; background: #a855f7; top: 45%; left: 55%; animation: d3 32s ease-in-out infinite; }\n  @keyframes d1 { 0%,100%{transform:translate(0,0)} 50%{transform:translate(60px,50px)} }\n  @keyframes d2 { 0%,100%{transform:translate(0,0)} 50%{transform:translate(-50px,70px)} }\n  @keyframes d3 { 0%,100%{transform:translate(-50%,-50%)} 50%{transform:translate(-40%,-58%)} }\n  .container { position: relative; z-index: 1; max-width: 1200px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 40px 24px; }\n  .header { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03); border: 1px solid var(--border); backdrop-filter: blur(12px); border-radius: 16px; padding: 40px; margin-bottom: 28px; position: relative; overflow: hidden; }\n  .header::before { content: ''; position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; height: 2px; background: linear-gradient(90deg, #22c55e, #3b82f6, #a855f7); }\n  .badges { display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 18px; flex-wrap: wrap; }\n  .badge { padding: 4px 12px; border-radius: 20px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.5px; }\n  .b-green { background: rgba(34,197,94,0.15); color: #22c55e; border: 1px solid rgba(34,197,94,0.3); }\n  .b-blue { background: rgba(59,130,246,0.15); color: #3b82f6; border: 1px solid rgba(59,130,246,0.3); }\n var(--border); }\n  .b-yellow { background: rgba(245,158,11,0.15); color: #f59e0b; border: 1px solid rgba(245,158,11,0.3); }\n  h1 { font-size: 26px; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 8px; }\n  .subtitle { color: var(--muted); font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.6; }\n  .section { background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--border); backdrop-filter: blur(12px); border-radius: 12px; padding: 24px 28px; margin-bottom: 20px; }\n  .sec-title { font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; color: var(--muted); margin-bottom: 18px; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; }\n  .sec-title::after { content: ''; flex: 1; height: 1px; background: var(--border); }\n  .grid-3 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3,1fr); gap: 14px; }\n  .grid-2 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 14px; }\n  @media(max-width:768px){ .grid-3,.grid-2 { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } }\n  .card { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.025); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 10px; padding: 16px; }\n  .card-head { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; margin-bottom: 8px; }\n  .dot { width: 9px; height: 9px; border-radius: 50%; flex-shrink: 0; }\n  .d-green { background: var(--green); box-shadow: 0 0 7px var(--green); }\n  .d-yellow { background: var(--yellow); box-shadow: 0 0 7px var(--yellow); }\n  .d-red { background: var(--red); box-shadow: 0 0 7px var(--red); }\n  .d-blue { background: var(--blue); box-shadow: 0 0 7px var(--blue); }\n  .card h4 { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; }\n  .card p { font-size: 12px; color: var(--muted); line-height: 1.5; margin-top: 4px; }\n  table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; }\n  th { font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.5px; color: var(--muted); padding: 9px 12px; text-align: left; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); }\n  td { padding: 9px 12px; font-size: 13px; border-bottom: 1px }\n  .t-green { background: rgba(34,197,94,0.12); color: #22c55e; }\n  .t-yellow { background: rgba(245,158,11,0.12); color: #f59e0b; }\n  .t-red { background: rgba(239,68,68,0.12); color: #ef4444; WP Rocket não gera cache estático em dev (sem config file)<br>\n            2. Mas o CloudFront de prod cacheará <code>/eventos-calendario/?tribe-bar-date=X</code> após o 1º request<br>\n            3. Para validar em dev: observar tempo de resposta consecutivo para mesma URL com query string\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"alert a-blue\">\n          <span class=\"icon\">ℹ️</span>\n          <div>\n            <strong>Pré-condição para deploy consistente:</strong><br>\n            Ao fazer <code>share deploy</code> de dev → prod, o <code>docker-dev.sh</code> agora chama automaticamente <code>rocket_generate_config_file()</code> — garantindo que o config file reflete as configurações atuais.\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n\n  <!-- Pendências manuais -->\n  <div class=\"section\">\n    <div class=\"sec-title\">Pendências — Requerem Decisão/Ação Manual</div>\n    <div class=\"grid-3\">\n      <div class=\"card\">\n        <div class=\"card-head\"><div class=\"dot d-yellow\"></div><h4>WAF P16 → Block</h4></div>\n        <p>0 hits nas últimas 24h.   "content": "---\nname: Concertação — TEC cache + deploy dev→prod\ndescription: Correções de cache WP Rocket, TEC query strings e scripts de deploy após incidente 2026-03-31\ntype: project\n---\n\nWP Rocket não gerava cache porque `wp-rocket-config/concertacaoamazonia.com.br.php` estava ausente. Resolvido com `rocket_generate_config_file()`.\n\nnginx precisava de `$rocket_https_suffix` baseado em `X-Forwarded-Proto` para servir `index-https.html` (WP Rocket HTTPS).\n\nTEC query strings (`tribe-bar-date`, `eventDisplay`, `tribe-bar-search`, `tribe_paged`) adicionados em: nginx bypass-exclusão, WP Rocket `cache_query_strings`, e banco dev.\n\n**Why:** CPU spikes em /eventos/ porque cada request com query string batia no PHP — sem cache.\n\n**How to apply:** Sempre que fazer deploy ou import de banco, verificar que `cache_query_strings` inclui os params TEC e chamar `rocket_generate_config_file()`.\n\nScripts bumped:\n- `ec2-deploy/post-deploy/14-renew-all-caches.sh` → v2.2.1 (rocket_generate_config_file adicionado)\n- `docker-dev/common/bin/docker-dev.sh` → v2.10.1 (idem no share deploy wrapper)\n- `wordpress/healthz-deep.php` — novo arquivo versionado no repo (PHP-FPM + Aurora + Redis phpredis nativo)\n"

## Auditoria concluída — Resumo executivo

**23 OK · 5 avisos · 0 falhas · 8 corrigidos automaticamente**

### O que foi corrigido nesta rodada

| Item | Onde | Status |
|------|------|--------|
| `cache_query_strings` TEC sincronizado em dev | Banco dev | ✓ |
| `healthz-deep.php` versionado no repo | `wordpress/healthz-deep.php` | ✓ |
| `14-renew-all-caches.sh` → v2.2.1 | ec2-deploy | ✓ `rocket_generate_config_file()` adicionado |
| `docker-dev.sh` → v2.10.1 | share deploy wrapper | ✓ `rocket_generate_config_file()` adicionado |
| 479 preload failures resetados | wp_wpr_rocket_cache | ✓ 4.555 completed |
| `X-XSS-Protection` removido | nginx security-headers.conf | ✓ CloudFront invalidado |
| mu-plugins site-specific | common/mu-plugins/ | Já estavam sincronizados |

### 3 pendências manuais (requerem decisão sua)

1. **WAF P16 Count → Block** — 0 hits/24h, seguro ativar após 2026-04-02
2. **X-Origin-Verify** — secret gerado (`cabf6ea2-...`), falta ativar no CloudFront console + nginx
3. **CSP completo** — requer audit separado com `Report-Only` em HML primeiro

### Deploy dev → prod agora é idempotente
Tanto o `share deploy` quanto o `14-renew-all-caches.sh` chamam `rocket_generate_config_file()` automaticamente — o config file do WP Rocket sempre refletirá o estado real do banco após qualquer deploy.
